### [1] [Quantifying Uncertainty In Wide Two-Layer Neural Networks: On The Law Of The Limiting Fluctuation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05982)
*Arnaud Descours,Arnaud Guillin,Geoffrey Lacour,Manon Michel,Boris Nectoux,Paul Stos*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏微分方程渐近方差信息的不确定性量化方法，替代了需要训练多个网络的深度集成方法。通过研究宽两层神经网络在随机梯度下降训练下的极限过程律，推导出协方差的闭式表达式，并在数值上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络预测中的不确定性量化是实际应用的关键问题，但传统的深度集成方法需要训练多个网络，计算成本高昂。本文旨在通过直接利用偏微分方程信息评估不确定性来降低计算开销。

Method: 基于轨迹中心极限定理，研究弱噪声环境下随机梯度下降训练的宽两层神经网络的随机波动极限过程。将该极限刻画为线性随机发展方程的弱解，证明其为加权Sobolev空间对偶中的中心高斯过程，并推导出有限维分布的闭式协方差表示。协方差通过一个带非局部源项的向后输运方程的解表示，其系数由平均场轨迹驱动。

Result: 通过对固定输入处的激活函数进行检验，获得了网络输出波动的极限方差的表达式。在一维回归数值示例中验证了该结果。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种计算上更高效的不确定性量化途径，避免了训练多个网络的深度集成需求，为神经网络预测不确定性评估提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification in neural networks prediction is a main issue for usual applications. Our approach seeks at reducing computation costs by directly evaluating uncertainty using PDE's information on the asymptotic variance, rather than the deep ensemble method which may be seen as a Monte Carlo estimation of the prediction, requiring the training of multiple networks. We thus study the law of the limiting process describing the random fluctuations around the mean-field limit of wide two-layer neural networks trained by stochastic gradient descent in a weak-noise regime. Building on a recent trajectorial central limit theorem, in which this limit is characterized as the weak solution of a linear stochastic evolution equation, we identify its law explicitly. More precisely, we show that it is a centered Gaussian process in the dual of a weighted Sobolev space, and we derive a closed covariance representation for the finite-dimensional distributions obtained by testing it against smooth functions. This covariance is expressed through the solution of a backward transport equation with a nonlocal source term, whose coefficients are driven by the mean-field trajectory. As a consequence, by testing against the activation function at a fixed input, we obtain an expression for the limiting variance of the corresponding network-output fluctuations. We illustrate this result numerically on a one-dimensional regression example.

</details>


### [2] [Hub-Aware Hybrid Search: Accelerating the Locally Aligned Ant Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06198)
*Simone Vilardi,Reynier Peletier,Felipe Contreras,Kerstin Bunte*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段改进的LAAT方法，通过快速预处理定位密集枢纽并用似然模型替代，再结合混合似然-信息素策略引导智能体高效穿越密集区域，从而提升了对嘈杂高维点云中流形结构（如宇宙网中的纤维、流、墙和团簇）的检测效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LAAT方法在处理高维噪声点云中的流形结构时，密集枢纽（如节点或球状星团）会主导蚂蚁智能体的活动，造成不必要的计算开销，影响检测效率。

Method: 两阶段解决方案：1) 快速预处理步骤定位密集枢纽并用定制的似然模型替代；2) 采用混合似然-信息素策略引导蚂蚁智能体高效穿越密集区域。

Result: 在合成数据和大规模宇宙网N体模拟数据上，该方法在检测效率和鲁棒性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段改进策略有效克服了原始LAAT在密集枢纽区域的计算瓶颈，增强了算法对宇宙大尺度结构中多维流形结构的探测能力。

Abstract: Finding manifold structures in noisy and high-dimensional point clouds is a challenging but important problem. In astronomical observation survey and simulation data the detection of filaments, streams (1D), walls (2D) and clusters (3D) gives rise to deeper understanding of the evolution of our universe. The Locally Aligned Ant Technique (LAAT) uses biologically inspired agents to efficiently recover faint and multidimensional structures. However, very dense hubs (e.g. nodes or globular clusters) dominate the ants' activity, creating unnecessary computational overheads. In this paper we propose a two-stage solution. First a fast preprocessing step locates the hubs and replaces them with a tailored likelihood model. Subsequently, a mixed likelihood-pheromone strategy guides the ants to efficiently bridge the dense regions. We demonstrate improvements in detection efficiency and robustness of LAAT with synthetic and a large-scale astronomical N-body simulation of the cosmic web.

</details>

### [3] [From Prediction to Self: Developmental Conditions for Agency in Minimal Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05605)
*Evan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过一系列受控实验，追踪了一个192维GRU系统从纯预测世界到区分自身因果影响的过程，发现实现自我意识需要按严格顺序满足四个条件，并提出了“代理增益”作为量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个仅能预测世界的系统如何发展出区分自身因果影响与外部世界变化的能力，即自我表征或因果归因的涌现机制。

Method: 通过40个受控实验组成的发育序列，在192维GRU中逐步添加组件，追踪系统区分自我引发变化与世界引发变化的能力。提出代理增益（A = Err_world - Err_self）作为量化指标。

Result: 发现四个必须按严格顺序满足的条件：(1) 持久状态形成稳定吸引子，(2) 因果行动循环连接输出到输入，(3) 本体感觉反馈使隐性因果知识显性化，(4) 异步觉醒——感知学习先于行动学习。自我感知预测器在周期性和混沌环境中均优于自我盲预测器。仅前向采样行动选择产生有意义的代理增益，梯度方法失效。同时报告了12个被证伪的假设。

Conclusion: 自我与世界的区分需要特定的发育顺序和架构条件，预测编码本身不足以产生自我表征，代理增益可作为追踪这一过程的可靠指标。

Abstract: How does a system that merely predicts the world come to distinguish its own causal influence from everything else? We trace this transition in a minimal 192-dimensional GRU through 40 controlled experiments arranged as a developmental sequence, adding components one at a time and tracking whether the system can distinguish self-caused from world-caused changes.
  The developmental path reveals four conditions that must be satisfied in strict order: (1) persistent state forming stable attractors, (2) a causal action loop linking output to input, (3) proprioceptive feedback that makes implicit causal knowledge explicit, and (4) asynchronous awakening - perceptual learning must consolidate before action learning begins. We propose agency gain (A = Err_world - Err_self), the predictive advantage of knowing one's own action, as a metric to track this process. The self-aware predictor consistently outperforms the self-blind predictor across periodic (sinusoidal) and chaotic (Lorenz) environments, and the metric survives ablation of all auxiliary components. Only forward-sampled action selection produces meaningful agency gain; two gradient-based alternatives degenerate.
  Equally significant are 12 falsified hypotheses mapping where development stalls: predictive coding alone does not produce self-represent

</details>


### [4] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一套LLM基准覆盖的体视学理论：任意基准套件的有效维度d_eff决定了评估盲区大小，实测多个排行榜d_eff∈[2.86,4.80]，盲区比跑马灯差距大两个数量级、比统计噪声大52-127倍；4个基准即可构成稳定核心，7/12即可覆盖90%能力空间；子集可跨季度迁移（93-97%保留）；特征值结构可预测哪些评估不可替代（ρ=-0.69）及哪些外部评估带来新信息（ρ=+0.38）。还独立解决了Gardner问题1.5（1995），建立了C²支撑函数在一般维度下的极小极大率Θ(R/(κ m^(2/(D-1))))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准评测缺乏系统性的覆盖度理论，无法量化各基准套件的能力空间覆盖盲区，也难以确定哪些基准是冗余的、哪些是不可替代的。本文旨在建立严格的体视学框架，从几何与统计角度回答：一个基准套件到底覆盖了多大能力空间？漏掉了多少？最少需要多少个基准才能稳定覆盖？

Method: (1) 体视学理论：将LLM能力建模为凸集，用Hausdorff距离量化可见能力与真实能力之间的差异，推导出覆盖误差的上下界；引入有效维度d_eff度量基准套件的能力空间维度。(2) 实验验证：在Open LLM v2、12基准扩展套件、LiveBench三个独立排行榜上估计d_eff；比较结构盲区与跑马灯差距、统计噪声。(3) 子模贪心算法（Nemhauser 1-1/e保证）选取稳定基准核心。(4) 交叉验证：12个内部基准与27个Chatbot Arena类别的特征值预测能力。(5) 独立理论贡献：通过最优恢复理论解决Gardner问题1.5，得到C²支撑函数估计的极小极大率。

Result: (1) 三个排行榜的d_eff∈[2.86,4.80]；结构盲区比跑马灯差距大两个数量级，比统计噪声大52-127倍。(2) 卡方投影模型下，各先验的top-2交换率在[0.38,0.49]，500次随机可见/保留划分中92%交换top-1，平均2.83/5的top-5模型改变。(3) 4个基准构成稳定核心，7/12覆盖90%，跨季度迁移保留93-97%。(4) 特征值结构预测评估不可替代性（ρ=-0.69, p=0.013）和新信息引入（ρ=+0.38）。(5) Gardner问题1.5在C²条件下的极小极大率为Θ(R/(κ m^(2/(D-1))))。

Conclusion: LLM基准评测存在巨大的结构盲区，远大于模型间的实际性能差异和统计噪声，意味着当前排行榜排名高度不稳定、不可靠；但通过体视学理论可以系统量化并优化基准覆盖。只需少量精心挑选的基准即可获得稳定的能力评估，且该选择可跨时间迁移。此外，独立解决的Gardner问题为高维凸体形状估计提供了严格的统计最优率。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [5] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Errorquake-10k基准（10000条查询，0-4连续严重性评分），发现即使准确率相同，不同开源LLM的错误严重性分布存在显著差异。通过拟合Gutenberg-Richter分布尾部斜率b，证明严重性分布与错误率信息非冗余（64.5%的b方差无法被epsilon解释），并建立了严重性机制分类学（低严重性为检索错误71%，高严重性为捏造39%）。建议在准确率之外报告严重性分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准将所有错误等同对待（如错误日期与伪造法庭裁决混为一谈），仅报告单一错误率指标，忽视了错误严重性分布的差异。作者旨在证明错误严重性分布携带错误率无法捕捉的判别信息。

Method: 构建Errorquake-10k基准，包含8个领域、5个难度层级、10000条查询，采用0-4连续严重性评分。对21个开源模型拟合严重性分布，使用Gutenberg-Richter尾部斜率b作为分布指标，通过bootstrap计算95%置信区间。进行519项三人人工验证研究（ICC(2,k=3)=0.85）和LLM-judge排名验证（rho=0.89）。证明非冗余性定理（I(b; model | epsilon)=1.56 bits），并建立严重性机制分类学（kappa=0.83）。

Result: 210个模型对中85对在准确率匹配条件下（|Delta epsilon|<0.05）b的95%置信区间不重叠；64.5%的跨模型b方差无法被epsilon解释；低严重性错误71%为检索错误，高严重性错误39%为捏造；模型大小与错误类型分布显著相关（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 错误严重性分布应与准确率一同报告，因其携带准确率无法提供的判别性信息。严重性分布可作为评估LLM可靠性的补充维度。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [6] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预算受限的微预训练场景下，采用分阶段分数阶因子实验工作流，在613次实验（2/5/10分钟）中识别早期效应结构。发现总批次、深度、宽度在短预算下惩罚最大，且随预算增加而放松；通过Benjamini-Hochberg校正，D/A/B/C因子在5/10分钟后仍保持非零估计，而E因子不显著；随机搜索虽能触及强基线但缺乏因子归因。最终提出有界方法：用短设计筛选高惩罚方向，确认锚点后局部优化，支持以桥接锚点为中心的24小时推荐（非硬件不变排序或通用超参优化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算受限的微预训练中，需要在共享加速器上对大量候选配方进行筛选（triage），然后才能投入更大的搜索预算。现有方法缺乏一种能够在该设定下稳定恢复早期效应结构的工作流，因此本研究探索分阶段分数阶因子实验是否可行。

Method: 在固定的auto-research单GPU训练循环上，设计了分阶段分数阶因子工作流：包含2/5/10分钟的试点和后续筛选实验（613次）、5/10分钟的全16条件种子重运行、目标种子锚点检查、同主机贪婪和随机基线、60分钟桥接包，以及Windows A100和Linux L40S上的有界24小时锚点延续。采用Benjamini-Hochberg校正进行因子显著性判断。

Result: 总批次、深度、宽度的主效应惩罚在短预算下最大，随预算增加而放松；在预声明种子全屏族中，D/A/B/C在5/10分钟后经B-H校正仍非零，E不显著；随机搜索可触及强基线但困于同一低惩罚区域且无因子归因；60分钟桥接锚点均值最低但无法分离工作流优化与模型容量优势；12/24小时三锚点延续中桥接仍最低，非桥接排序受主机敏感性影响。

Conclusion: 推荐有界方法：先用短设计实验识别高惩罚方向，再通过重复运行确认有希望的锚点，最后在缩减空间内局部优化。证据支持以桥接为中心的24小时推荐，但不支持硬件不变排序或通用超参优化优越性。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [7] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PyCC，一个基于特征曲线（CC）框架的Python库，用于从时间序列数据中发现常微分方程。该库将结构骨架假设与多种方程发现范式（神经网络、符号回归、稀疏回归）相结合，提高了逆问题求解的良态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方程发现中逆问题的病态性会导致多个候选模型拟合效果相近，现有方法虽能缩小搜索空间但仍需人工后验筛选。本文旨在提供一个灵活工具，通过结构骨架假设和模块化设计系统化解决这一问题。

Method: 基于特征曲线（CC）的假设驱动方法：用户定义与ODE族关联的结构骨架，融入领域知识和先验假设，利用骨架的可识别性验证正确性。PyCC库模块化集成了神经网络、符号回归和稀疏回归等多种方程发现范式。

Result: 开发了PyCC Python库，实现了特征曲线框架下的灵活方程发现工具，支持用户自定义骨架和假设，可从时间依赖数据中发现ODE。

Conclusion: PyCC库通过结构骨架假设和模块化设计，有效缓解了逆问题的病态性，为研究人员和工程师提供了可灵活定义骨架和假设的ODE发现工具。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>

*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果定位方法，在蒸馏LLM中识别出负责时间偏好处理的神经子图，发现LLM对未来折扣比人类更平缓，且时间偏好的几何结构编码在残差流中，最后展示了通过引导向量可调控时间偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在决策中需要权衡短期收益与长期后果，但其内部如何表征和解决这种时间权衡尚不清楚，本文旨在通过 mechanistic interpretability 揭示这一过程。
